- **commercial-credit-memo**: Truist commercial credit memo methodology. Section structure, ratio definitions, NAICS-aggregated peer analysis, covenant compliance framework, Truist-branded HTML/PDF output. Use whenever drafting, refreshing, or auditing a commercial credit memo for a C&I borrower at Truist Bank — covers C&I working capital lines, term loans, revolvers, and middle-market deals up to roughly $250M facility size. Trigger on phrases like "draft a credit memo", "refresh the memo", "covenant compliance", "risk rating writeup", or any request to produce or update a commercial loan underwriting document.
